Financial Report Source 29

Document 007: 10月进出口数据点评：AI量价分化延续，美欧经贸格局生变

Source PDF: all report pdf/10月进出口数据点评：AI量价分化延续，美欧经贸格局生变.pdf
Report source: 国信证券
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

事项：
11 月 10日，海关总署发布进出口数据。我国 10 月出口（以美元计价，后同）同比增长 35.2%；进口同比
增长 41.6%，贸易顺差 1052.77亿美元。
评论：
结论：
10月外贸增速维持高位，电子产业链支撑较强，价格因素推升名义增长。出口同比增长 35.2%，较上月小
幅回落，1—10 月累计增长 22.7%；进口同比增长 41.6%，较上月加快 10.6 个百分点，1—10 月累计增长
28.9%。我们测算，集成电路和自动数据处理设备及零部件合计拉动出口增长 13.6个百分点，仍是外贸高
景气度的核心因素。
出口方面，电子产品支撑较强，传统品类表现分化，AI 品类出口增速量价背离值得关注。10 月出口有三
点变化值得注意，第一，电子产业链仍是出口增长的核心支撑，但金额高增主要依靠均价抬升。集成电路、
计算机相关产品出口金额分别增长 137.1%和 114.8%；但集成电路出口数量反而下降 2.5%，隐含均价上涨
143.2%，若后续涨价放缓、同期基数抬升，名义增速或面临回落压力。第二，传统制造出口仍有亮点，但

增长动能有所分化。通用机械、汽车零配件出口分别增长 34.5%和 32.1%，较上月加快；汽车、家电和玩
具则分别增长 37.6%、20.9%和 27.5%，增速有所回落，传统品类的改善尚不均衡。第三，成品油出口同比
保持高增，环比下行，原因在于国内保供扰动短期发运。月初炼厂为补充国内库存暂停部分出口、假期后
恢复安排，导致出口数量环比下降 27.6%。
进口方面，电子与原材料共同支撑高增，价格贡献较大，实物需求表现分化。一方面，电子产品进口保持
高景气，亚洲产业链贸易活跃。集成电路、计算机相关产品进口金额分别增长 78.3%和 261.0%；自韩国、
东盟进口分别增长 110.2%和 42.0%，相关产业链仍是进口增长的重要支撑。另一方面，部分原材料及汽车
进口仍偏弱，需求恢复存在结构性差异。铁矿砂进口金额下降 11.5%，铜矿砂进口数量下降 11.6%；汽车
及零配件进口金额分别下降 38.5%和 41.7%。
往后看，AI 产业链仍将支撑外贸韧性，但价格效应减弱与贸易政策调整，可能推动出口增速回落。此外，

随着中期选举结束、中欧经贸磋商落地，我国与美欧之间的贸易关系将出现变化。中美经贸存在维持阶段
性稳定的空间，科技领域竞争仍可能趋紧。民主党赢得两院后，特朗普推进政策将面临更多国会制约；我
们判断，稳物价、稳经济的诉求可能增加双方维持谈判的动力，但行政端贸易措施仍有反复风险。与此同
时，对华科技限制具有较强的两党共识，先进芯片、半导体设备和 AI 相关出口管制仍可能收紧，电子产
业链将同时面对需求支撑与政策约束。中欧经贸磋商后，混动汽车出口数量约束将限制相关品类增长，对
欧出口面临“政策确定性改善、数量扩张受限”的双重影响。

### Page 2

图1：外贸量值表
资料来源：Wind、国信证券经济研究所整理
 出口：高增延续，AI品类量价背离
10 出口同比保持较高增速。出口同比保持较高增速，但 9 月冲高后的环比回落值得关注。10 月出口同比
增长 35.2%，较上月小幅放缓，1—10 月累计增长 22.7%；当月金额环比下降 7.8%。国庆假期及节前后集
中发运可能影响月度节奏。外需仍处扩张区间，新增订单边际降温。10 月全球制造业 PMI 为 52.6，较 9
月的 53.0回落；中国制造业新出口订单指数由 50.0降至 48.7。外需尚有支撑、增量动能有所放缓。
从商品结构上看，电子产品高增更多体现在金额端，数量出现收缩。集成电路、自动数据处理设备及其零
部件出口金额分别增长 137.1%和 114.8%，但集成电路数量同比下降 2.5%，金额与数量走势背离。汽车、
家电和玩具出口增速分别回落至 37.6%、20.9%和 27.5%；通用机械、汽车零配件则加快至 34.5%和 32.1%，
传统制造出口并非全面走弱。汽车出口数量同比仍增长 33.5%。

从国别结构上看，亚洲贸易联系仍较活跃，欧美市场增速有所分化。对韩国出口同比增长 92.4%，较上月
明显加快；对东盟增长 46.3%，虽有放缓，仍保持较高增速。对美国出口增长 32.9%，较上月回落；对欧
盟增长 24.3%，总体变化较小。国别表现与电子产业链景气具有一致性。
成品油同比仍强，国内保供放大了环比发运波动。10月成品油出口金额同比增长 80.3%，数量增长 14.6%、
隐含均价上涨 57.3%；数量环比则下降 27.6%。这主要是因为炼厂为补充国内库存，月初暂停部分成品油
出口。

 进口：消费品进口偏弱，加工贸易特征明显
电子产业链继续拉动进口。10 月集成电路、自动数据处理设备及其零部件进口金额分别增长 78.3%和
261.0%，体现相关产业链贸易的活跃度。原油进口金额同比增速由 3.3%升至 41.0%。成品油进口金额增长

### Page 4

45.2%。铜进口量价均有支撑，但价格及结构因素占主导。与此同时，铁矿砂、汽车进口金额分别下降 11.5%
和 38.5%。进口结构仍有分化。
进口来源地同样呈现亚洲较强、欧洲偏弱的特征。自韩国、东盟进口分别增长 110.2%和 42.0%，自日本进
口增长 26.5%，均较上月加快；自欧盟进口则下降 4.2%。这一结构与电子产业链贸易较强相呼应，也说明
进口提速具有较明显的区域与行业集中性。

115 年 9 月海關進出口貿易初步統計 
一、進出口貿易變動 
（一）出口 
9 月出口 872.2 億美元，創歷年單月新高，較上月增 5.9%，較上年
同月增 60.9%(按新臺幣計算增 68.2%)；第3 季出口 2,449.2 億美元，
較上年同季增 44.6%；累計 1 至 9 月 6,615.5 億美元，較上年同期增
46.2%(按新臺幣計算增 49.1%)。 
（二）進口 
9 月進口 635.9 億美元，亦創歷年單月新高，較上月增 5.8%，較上
年同月增 51.7%(按新臺幣計算增 58.6%)；第 3 季進口 1,818.0 億美
元，較上年同季增 44.3%；累計 1 至 9 月 5,010.0 億美元，較上年同
期增 41.3%(按新臺幣計算增 43.8%)。 
（三）出入超 
9 月出超 236.3 億美元，為單月新高，較上年同月增 113.4 億美元；
累計 1 至 9 月出超 1,605.5 億美元，較上年同期增 625.4 億美元。

二、出口主要貨品 
（一）與上年同月比較 
由於創新科技應用商機持續擴展，推升相關供應鏈出貨動能，加上
消費性電子新品備貨需求與產品價格上漲效應之挹注， 9 月出口較
上年同月增 330.0 億美元(+60.9%)，主要變動如下： 
1. 資通與視聽產品出口 391.3 億美元(歷年單月次高)， 較上年同月增
198.6 億美元(+1.0 倍)， 其中電腦及其附屬單元增157.4 億美元(+1.1
倍)、儲存媒體增 17.6 億美元(+2.2 倍)、交換器及路由器增 10.5
億美元(+1.1 倍)。 
2. 電子零組件出口 311.3 億美元(歷年單月次高)， 較上年同月增97.1
億美元(+45.3%)，其中積體電路增 93.9 億美元(+46.1%)、印刷電
路增 1.4 億美元(+33.7%)、 電容器及電阻器增0.8 億美元(+32.0%)。 
3. 礦產品出口 22.6 億美元，較上年同月增 10.5 億美元(+86.4%)，其
中石油煉製品增 10.1 億美元(+99.0%)。 
4. 機械出口 27.4 億美元(歷年單月次高)，較上年同月增 6.4 億美元
(+30.7%)，其中生產半導體等機械增 2.7 億美元(+61.0%)、未列名
具特殊功能機械增 0.6 億美元(+73.6%)。 
5. 基本金屬及其製品出口 26.9 億美元，較上年同月增 5.0 億美元
(+22.9%)，其中銅及其製品增 2.5 億美元(+65.1%)、鋼鐵及其製品
增 1.4 億美元(+11.7%)。

三、進口主要貨品 
（一）與上年同月比較 
隨 AI 產業鏈全球分工與出口引申需求擴增，電子零組件、資通產品
及半導體設備進口維持強勁，加上國際農工原料行情處於高檔，9
月進口較上年同月增 216.7 億美元(+51.7%)，主要變動如下： 
1. 農工原料進口440.5 億美元， 較上年同月增163.9億美元(+59.3%)，
其中電子零組件增 112.6 億美元(+82.5%)、電腦之零附件增 19.4
億美元(+1.5 倍)、礦產品增 17.1 億美元(+38.9%)。 
2. 資本設備進口 128.0 億美元， 較上年同月增 36.0 億美元(+39.1%)，
其中資通設備增 18.4 億美元(+75.0%)、半導體設備增 12.4 億美元
(+34.9%)，交通運輸設備減 1.4 億美元(-46.4%)。 
3. 消費品進口 62.1 億美元，較上年同月增 16.5 億美元(+36.0%)，其
中固態非揮發性儲存裝置增 16.4 億美元(+5.3 倍)、筆電增 0.5 億
美元(+43.8%)，藥粧用品減 1.2 億美元(-16.6%)。

七、綜合分析
（一）受惠於人工智慧(AI)、高效能運算及雲端服務等應用商機持續擴展，
加以步入傳統外銷旺季 、產品價格上漲效應，9 月出口 872.2 億美元，
年增 60.9%，連續 35 個月正成長；進口635.9 億美元，年增51.7%，
主因 AI 產業鏈國際分工與出口引申需求擴張，及國際原物料行情居
高；出、進口值及出超 236.3 億美元齊創單月新高。累計 1 至 9 月出
口年增 46.2%、進口增 41.3%。
（二）9 月主要貨品出口普遍較上年同月成長，以資通與視聽產品增 1.0 倍
最佳，包括顯示卡、伺服器、儲存媒體、 交換器及路由器等出口均
呈倍數增加 ；其次為礦產品增 86.4%，主因全球油品供應偏緊支撐價
格維持高檔；電子零組件隨 AI 應用及消費性電子新品備貨需求升溫，
增 45.3%，機械、電機產品亦各增 3 成上下。累計 1 至 9 月資通與視
聽產品、電子零組件出口併計增 59.5%，占總出口比重 79.0%。

（三）主要市場方面，9 月對美國、東協出口值分創歷年單月次高、新高，
年增率各為 1.1 倍及 93.4%，合計對總出口增加貢獻達 7 成；對中國
大陸與香港、歐洲、日本出口各年增 30.2%、20.6%、13.4%。累計 1
至 9 月對五大市場出口值皆為歷年同期最高，其中對美國、東協均
提前刷新全年紀錄 ， 分別年增59.3%、52.9%，對歐洲出口年增48.8%，
對陸港、日本各增 28.5%、26.6%。
（四）展望未來，全球貿易政策及地緣政治情勢變化等，仍增添總體 經濟
不確定性，惟隨人工智慧應用 持續深化，國際雲端服務業者 及各國
加速 AI 基礎建設投資，帶動半導體、伺服器等相關供應鏈需求擴增，
加以科技新品備貨、中國大陸及歐美年底採購旺季將屆， 均有利挹
注我國外銷動能，第 4 季出口可望延續成長態勢。

Document 034: 2026-11-11_［みずほ経済インサイト］堅調なAI需要で大幅増益が視野_1ea94428

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026-11-11_［みずほ経済インサイト］堅調なAI需要で大幅増益が視野_1ea94428.pdf
Report source: Mizuho
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

1

堅調なAI 需要で大幅増益が視野 
投資増につながるかが焦点に

○ 製造業の業績見通しは大幅に改善している。輸出向けを中心とするAI関連財の需要拡大が背景。
2026年度は好業績が期待され、二桁増益が視野に入る 
○ AI関連財の増産を背景に、国内製造業の設備不足感は2025年後半以降強まっている。足元では関
連業種の機械受注が増加しており、AI需要拡大が国内設備投資に波及する兆しがみられる 
○ 設備投資は緩やかな拡大が息長く続く見通し。ただし、米国でデータセンター建設規制が実施さ
れれば、輸出・業績・設備投資の好循環が逆回転に転じるリスクも

１．企業景況感は AI 関連の製造業を中心に改善 
AIの学習・利用に必要なデータセンター投資や半導体需要が世界的に堅調であることを背景に、日
本の製造業の景況感は幅広く改善している。日銀短観（2026年9月調査）では、大企業・製造業の業況
判断DIが6月調査から改善した（図表1）。業種別では、半導体や電子部品などを含む電気機械や、半
導体製造装置を含む生産用機械の改善が際立つ。 そのほか、窯業・土石製品、 非鉄金属、半導体材料・
素材を扱う化学などでも改善がみられた。ロイター短観（10月調査）でも、「半導体関連市場の需要
が強い」との声が散見され、AI関連需要の堅調さがうかがえる。 
AI関連財の輸出が拡大し、足元で企業の業績見通しは大きく改善している。企業業績の改善を国内
の需要拡大につなげるうえで、次の焦点となるのが設備投資である。企業業績の改善に比べて、投資
図表 1 業況判断 DI 図表 2 日本の AI 関連財輸出額

る。中国の春節要因を均すため、1・2月は両者の伸びの
平均値。DCはデータセンターの略

### Page 2

2

などの内需への恩恵はまだ明確ではなく、今後設備投資の増加として発現する可能性が期待される。 
２．AI 関連財輸出は好調。2026 年度の製造業は二桁増益が視野に 
世界的なAIサービスの普及に伴い、データセンターや関連機器への投資が拡大している。大規模な
データセンターを保有・運営し、クラウドサービスやAI向けの計算基盤を提供するIT企業、とりわけ
ハイパースケーラーと呼ばれる大規模事業者を中心に、高水準の設備投資を継続する見通しが示され
ていることを踏まえると、AI関連投資の高い伸びは当面持続する見込みである。こうしたなか、モデ
ルの演算を担う処理装置のほか、大量のデータを蓄える記憶装置、サーバー同士をつなぐ通信ケーブ
ル、電力を安定供給する電源装置などといった財において投資需要が高まっている。日本企業が供給
する財についても、電子部品・機器への需要が増加しているほか、AI向け半導体の増産投資は日本の
半導体製造・検査装置や材料への需要を押し上げている。 
図表2は、輸出全体から、WTOの定義に従ってAI関連財を抽出したものだ。AI関連財輸出は2025年終
盤以降加速しており、8月時点では前年比＋29.1％と高い伸びになった。主因は半導体・電子部品で、
旺盛な需要のもとで半導体価格が大幅に上昇し、輸出金額を押し上げている。また、半導体製造・検
査機器や、データセンターに組み込まれる機械 ・ 部材、 半導体の製造に用いられる原材料 ・ 素材など、
幅広いAI関連財が輸出増に寄与している。 
輸出額の高い伸びを受け、製造業では業績見通しが改善している。 日銀短観（9月調査）の2026年度
経常利益計画（全規模・製造業）は、6月調査時点から大幅に上方修正され、前年度比＋11.6％の増益
が見込まれる（図表3）。特に電気機械では、上期の堅調な輸出実績を反映して、経常利益計画が大き
く上方修正された。このほか、業況判断DIの改善が目立った業種でも同様の傾向がみられた。堅調な
AI関連需要が下期も続けば、製造業では大幅増益が期待される。

３．AI 関連業種の投資需要は増勢加速の兆しも 
今後の実質GDPへの影響を見通すうえでの焦点は、 企業収益の拡大が設備投資を中心とした内需の押
し上げにどの程度繋がるかだ。この点、AI需要の追い風を受け好業績が見込まれることを踏まえれば、
設備投資の伸びはやや物足りなさが否めない。 日銀短観 （9月調査）によると、 製造業の2026年度設備
投資計画は直近5年間では最も低い伸びに留まっている（図表4）。建材や生産設備の価格上昇、金利
上昇に伴う資金調達コストの増加が投資負担となり、好業績のもとでも計画の積み増しを抑えている
可能性がある。 
もっとも、設備投資需要は計画対比で伸び余地がある。AI関連財を中心に増産が進むなか、能力増
強投資の必要性が高まっているためだ。AI関連財の生産は2025年秋以降、はっきりとした増加傾向が
続いている（図表5）。生産・営業用設備判断DI（全規模・製造業）も2025年後半から低下しており、
製造業の設備不足感は強まっている（図表6）。 
実際、AI需要の拡大が今後の設備投資を押し上げる兆しもみられる。 図表7は、設備投資の先行指標
とされる機械受注（国内需要向け）を需要者別に分解し、価格変動の影響を取り除いたものだ。これ
をみると2025年夏場以降、情報通信機械、電気機械、はん用・生産用機械といった業種からの受注が
急増していることがわかる。結果として、半導体製造装置をはじめとするAI・データセンター関連品
目では受注残高が積み上がっている（図表8）。 
ただし、機械受注が設備投資として顕在化するまでにはタイムラグを伴うとみられる。そもそも、
受注残高が積み上がっている背景には、旺盛な需要だけでなく、供給面の制約による影響があると考
えられる1。このため、設備投資の増加ペースは緩やかなものにとどまる公算が大きいとみているが、
その分、今後は息の長い投資拡大局面が続くだろう。

図表 5 鉱工業生産 図表6 生産・営業用設備判断 DI

### Page 4

4

４．今後は政府の投資支援策も民間投資を後押し。米国の規制強化リスクには要注意 
以上のように、先行きの設備投資は、設備不足感の強まるAI関連産業の投資需要に支えられ、緩や
かに増勢を強めると見込まれる。 
また今後は、政府の政策支援が、投資コストの負担軽減を通じて、設備投資を後押しするとみられ
る。7月末に始動した政府の「大胆な投資促進税制」では、一定の要件を満たす国内投資について、即
時償却や税額控除の適用を認める減税措置が設けられた。さらに、2027年度予算の編成では、特別予
算枠として『「強く豊かな日本」投資枠』を設け、民間投資を促す支援策が検討されている。具体的
な支援策が整えば、複数年度にわたる投資計画の実行を支えよう。 
なお、11月3日に投開票された米国中間選挙の結果を受け、米国におけるデータセンター投資が下振
れるリスクにも触れておきたい。同選挙では、データセンター建設計画の多いテキサス州やジョージ
ア州の州知事選などにおいて、建設の一時停止を掲げる民主党候補が勝利した。米国では、データセ
ンター建設規制に関して州政府や地方自治体が大きな権限を持つ。 
実際、市場ではAI関連企業の業績・投資下振れが意識され、グローバルにハイテク銘柄の株価が急
落した。仮に今後、米国でデータセンター建設が規制されれば、日本でも半導体・電子部品や製造装
置などの輸出が減少し、企業業績が下押しされる可能性がある。その際、AI関連財が減産に転じて設
備不足感が弱まれば、日本国内における能力増強投資の需要も後退しかねない。新知事の任期開始お
よび新議会の招集は2027年1月だが、当面の米国政治動静については注意深くみていく必要があろう。

1 なお、供給制約に起因して、資本財の輸入浸透度（国内供給に占める輸入品の割合）が一層上昇する可能性には留意が必要で
ある。今後、国内の供給余力が限られるなかで投資需要が一層輸入品に向かえば、国産資本財への需要が減退し、投資需要増に
よる GDP の押し上げ効果が限られるリスクがある。

金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 19 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國 9 月通膨升溫，Fed 年底前再升息機率仍高： 美國 9 月 CPI 年增 3.7%，高於 8 月的
3.4%；核心 CPI 年增率亦由 2.4%升至 2.6%，顯示通膨壓力不只來自能源，核心價格亦有回
升。隔日的 PPI 年增率則由 5.6%升至 6.3%，反映上游成本壓力仍強。通膨重新升溫，使Fed
即使面對就業降溫，也難以快速轉向寬鬆，市場對年底前再度升息的預期仍高。
◼ 台積電財報創新高，AI 需求強勁但市場轉向檢視融資風險：台積電 10/15 公布其第三季淨利

達 7,631 億元、年增 69%，連續第六季刷新紀錄，第三季美元營收 467.2 億美元，並預估第
四季營收將升至 523～543 億美元 ； 公司同時將2026 全年營收成長預估提高至約 44%， 反映
AI 晶片與先進製程需求仍極為強勁。然而，高利率環境使市場開始檢視 AI 資料中心業者的槓
桿與融資能力，CoreWeave 約 500 億美元債務引發信用疑慮，引發市場波動。
◼ 中東衝突再度推高能源風險 ， 油價高檔加劇全球通膨壓力 ：胡塞武裝上週攻擊沙烏地阿美設施 ，
伊朗亦表示採取新的海上攻擊策略，使荷莫茲海峽與波斯灣航運風險再度升高。10/16 布蘭特
原油與西德洲原油期貨雙雙收在近三週高點。
市場展望
◼ 科技股中期趨勢料將不變 ， 但短線波動勢將加劇 ：近期市場同時面臨通膨升溫 、AI 資本支出疑
慮與重要科技財報公佈在即等不確定因素，短線風險偏好可能受到壓抑。在利率維持高檔及市
場評價偏高的環境下，市場短線料將隨財報結果與市場預期而出現較大波動。然而，上週台積
電與 ASML 財報結果整體優於預期 ， 可見AI 相關企業營運與資本支出尚未出現全面轉弱跡象 ，

科技產業基本面仍具一定支撐，惟後續宜更加重視企業獲利、現金流及資本支出變動及科技子
產業的表現分化現象。
◼ 中天期美債與投資級債債券兼顧息收與利率風險：美國經濟仍具韌性 ， 加上政府與企業發債供
給維持高檔，可能使市場利率持續處於較高水準。雖然投資級債信用利差位於相對低檔，但企
業獲利、現金流及償債能力仍具支撐，加上較高的美國公債基準利率，使投資級債的利息收益
仍具吸引力。相較之下，短天期債券面臨未來利率下降後的再投資風險，長天期債券則較容易
受到公債供給增加 、 期限溢酬及利率波動影響 ， 因此中天期美國公債與投資債券較能兼顧息收
與利率風險。

Key Data  _____________________________________

GS Forecast  __________________________________

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

Co-Tech’s 3Q26 core business (operating income) was 7/5% lower 
than GSe/BBG consensus, mainly due to higher summer electricity 
rates which led to a 0.7ppt QoQ decrease in 3Q26 GPM, and came in 
1.4/1.5ppt below GSe/BBG consensus. Product mix continues to 
improve with HVLP3+ copper contribution at close to 20% (vs. 
15-20% in 2Q26). Co-Tech’s OPEX increased to NT$90mn (vs. 
NT$83mn in 2Q26). NI was 8/4% lower than GSe/BBG consensus 
mainly due to the lower than expected GM.  
For 4Q26, we expect Co-Tech’s revenue will increase by 8% QoQ, 
mainly driven by (1) price hikes of ~10% QoQ across all copper foil 
products (we saw price hike starting to accelerate from late June, 
and we expect to see prices increase to further accelerate in 4Q26), 
(2) initial shipment of higher ASP/margin next gen products, 
including HVLP5 and RG313+, and (3) higher HVLP3+ copper foil 
contribution due to an increase in capacity (HVLP3+ capacity could 
reach 250 tons/month by end of 2026 vs. 200-250 tons/month in 
3Q26). On GM, we expect the 4Q26 level to be 31.4% (4.7ppt QoQ 
increase), mainly due to the improved product mix while the 
summer electricity rates impact comes to an end (the impact ended 
by mid-October). 
CB issuance plan announced to fund further capacity expansion 
in Malaysia: Co-Tech announced on Nov 6 its plan to issue CB 
(Convertible Bond) of up to NT$7bn, supporting the company’s 
Plant 4 expansion in Malaysia, which the company is expected to 
spend NT$7-10bn on two phases, with each phase expected to add 
~800tons/month of effective HVLP4+ capacity (based on yield of 
50-60%). The two phases are targeted to reach MP by end of 
2028/29, respectively. Full conversion of the CB would result in less 
than 10% EPS dilution per GSe (based on the average share price 
over the past week), while each phase of the expansion could 
contribute NT$11bn+ in revenue at full operation based on our 
estimate (vs. Co-Tech’s 2025 revenue of NT$7.9bn).

Co-Tech Development Corp. (8358.TWO)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM

### Page 3

Investment view - Capacity expansion extended into 2029, still insufficient to meet 
with client demand 
We maintain our positive view on Co-Tech’s revenue and profitability outlook, 
underpinned by (1) a substantial capacity ramp-up, with Co-Tech’s high-end capacity 
will increase by 3.5x+/75%+/50%+ by 2027/28/29 end (vs. 2026 end), outpacing the 
expansion expected from other major competitors and supporting further market share 
gains, and (2) supply condition for high-end copper foil remains tight, supporting a 
better pricing environment, and we expect price increase to accelerate in coming 
quarters/years. 
We continue to believe Co-Tech is well-positioned to benefit from the structural demand 
for high end copper foil in a shortage environment, as the company has announced 
capacity expansion plans for a new Plant 4 in Malaysia (see here), with capacity already 
secured by multiple customers. The company now targets its monthly HVLP3+ effective 
capacity to reach 833/1666/2500 tons/month by 2027/28/29 end (vs. current HVLP3+ 
effective capacity of 200-250 tons/month). Despite the proactive capacity expansion, 
the planned capacity additions remain insufficient to fully meet with demand from key 
customers. This should enable the company to further expand market share amid high 
end copper foil supply shortage environment, and we expect the company’s HVLP3+ 
market share to further expand to 41/53% in 2027/28E (vs. 19% in 2026E).  
We expect high-end copper foil TAM to reach US$2.7bn by 2028E (Exhibit 1, with 133% 
2025-28E CAGR), following our update on global AI PCB/CCL TAM (see here). We now 
believe the shortage ratio for HVLP3+ industry to be 39-48% in 2026-28E (Exhibit 4, 
slightly lower than our previous estimate, which was 45-55% in 2026-28E, as we factor 
in the latest capacity expansion plans), with the shortage already starting in 2H26, as the 
ramp-up of mainstream AI server projects increasingly adopts HVLP4+ copper foil. The 
wide shortage ratio would suggest an elevated industry UTR and provide further support 
for price increase over the coming quarters/years.  
We maintain Buy on Co-Tech, with a new TP of NT$990 from NT$940.

22x 2028E EPS, which is +1SD above the company’s upcycle valuation in the past

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - Co-Tech 
Co-Tech is a global leading general server copper foil supplier, with ~60% market share 
in 2025 per GSe. We expect the company to enjoy solid share gain opportunities in the 
near term, considering its proactive HVLP capacity expansion plan and strong 
know-how in the advanced copper foil industry. Also, adoption of HVLP4 copper foil 
from AI servers and 1.6T networking switches starting in 2H26 should be a key demand 
driver for Co-Tech, in our view. Additionally, the company’s proactive pricing strategy 
and ability to improve production yield should drive revenue momentum in the long 
term.  
We are Buy rated as we believe Co-Tech will see better revenue/profitability in the long 
term given its shift to a more premium product mix along with proactive capacity plans 
(Plant 3 to be ready by end of 2027 and Plant 4 phase 1 & 2 to be ready by end of 
2028/29) and this should lead to strengthened valuations.

Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$990 is based on a 22x 2028E P/E, which is 
+1SD above the company’s average upcycle valuation in the past 10-years. 
Key downside risks: (1) earlier than expected CPO adoption in the computing IC, (2) 
slower than expected yield rate improvement speed, and (3) much stronger than 
expected capacity growth from HVLP peers, limiting pricing power

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

### Page 1

M
Update
Broader recovery supports record earnings. 3Q revenue was NT$597.3bn (+1% Q/
Q, +82% Y/Y), with gross margin of 3.6% (+0.2ppt Q/Q, -0.1ppt Y/Y, 0.3ppt above
MSe), and net profit of NT$10.3bn (+6% Q/Q, +170% Y/Y, 42% above MSe). Data-
center revenue fell 5% Q/Q on shipment timing, while industrial, automotive and
Future Electronics revenue increased sequentially. The richer mix lifted gross margin
19bp Q/Q to 3.60%; operating margin rose 18bp to 2.38%, with operating expenses

increasing only 2% Q/Q. Net financing costs rose 22% Q/Q to NT$1.94bn.
AI drives a stronger 4Q. Management guides 4Q revenue of NT$730-770bn, with
the NT$750bn midpoint up 26% Q/Q, as data-center and communications growth
outweigh seasonal declines across consumer applications, industrial and automotive.
The guided gross-margin midpoint falls 36bp Q/Q to 3.24% on mix, but operating
profit rises 19% Q/Q to NT$16.96bn as expenses grow only 1%. 4Q EPS guidance is

NT$8.07–9.05, with the NT$8.56 midpoint up 7% Q/Q. Financing costs of NT$2.6–
2.8bn and the larger weighted-average share base temper the translation from
operating-profit growth into EPS.
Growth broadens, but cash conversion matters. Future Electronics grew over 40%
Y/Y in 3Q and has started shipping data-center products. Management remains
positive on 2027 AI demand, without providing a full-year earnings forecast.
Inventory days increased Q/Q from 47 to 66  and net debt from NT$27.6bn to NT

$46.4bn, although longer payables kept the cash-conversion cycle near 19 days
versus 18 in 2Q. We see further earnings potential from AI scale and Future’s
recovery, with funding requirements and inventory conversion the key execution
risks.

Valuation methodology
We cut our price target by 15% to  NT$296: We update our residual income model to
reflect earnings estimate revisions. We increase our equity risk premium assumption from
11.8% to 12.5% to reflect segment volatilities, and reduce our intermediate growth rate
assumption from 10% to 8% to reflect a more conservative medium-term growth forecast.
We keep other key RI assumptions unchanged: 2.0% risk-free rate, 0.9 beta, cash dividend

payout ratio of 85%, and a long-term growth rate of 3.0%. Bull case PT is cut from NT
$406 to NT$344.5, implying 10.6x 2027 P/E. Bear case PT is cut from NT$230 to NT$160,
implying 4.9x 2027 P/E. The risk reward is now skewed more towards the bear case due to
potential downstream demand disruption.
Exhibit 3: WT: Residual Income Model
Source: Morgan Stanley Research estimates

forward P/E since 2018, which we see as fair given improving demand and the company's
continued progress in penetrating the AI supply chain.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Quanta Computer (2382.TW)

Quanta Computer
Company description
Founded in 1988, Quanta Computer is a Taiwan-based original design
manufacturer (ODM) of notebook (NB) PCs and other electronic hardware. It
is the world's largest NB ODM in terms of shipments, with major customers
including the leading Taiwan, US, and Japanese electronics brands. In
addition to notebooks, Quanta is expanding into cloud-related product
development, which accounted for 30-35% of total revenues in 2022.

Investment strategy
We rate Quanta at Buy. We expect stabilizing NB shipment, solid server sales
growth amid increasing AI-related applications, as well as rising EV
component exposure to drive better margins and stronger earnings growth.
The company’s attractive cash yield should also help support the shares on
the downside in case of market volatility.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$450 is based on a 14x PE multiple on 2027E earnings.
The target multiple is in line with historical trading average since 2021, mainly

to reflect strong earnings growth trajectory brought about by servers/AI
servers and EV components. We use a P/E-based methodology as we believe
the market will be primarily focused on Quanta's earnings performance given
the dynamics of the NB/server industry.
Risks
Downside risks that could prevent the stock from reaching our target price
include: 1) slower-than-expected server/AI server demand growth; 2)
execution risks relating to margin improvement such as production yield rate

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

Additional supply enquiries point to a potential revenue opportunity for EMC.
Our supply chain checks indicate that PCB makers supplying NVIDIA (covered by
Joseph Moore) have approached EMC (2383.TW, OW) for additional Copper Clad
Laminates (CCL) supply support for VR200 switch boards (32-layer PCBs). If these
enquiries translate into incremental orders, we estimate the resulting revenue

contribution would represent a single-digit percentage of EMC’s total revenue.
We expect EMC to retain a majority share of CCL supply for VR200 switch
boards. Our positive view reflects EMC’s technological capabilities and our
preference for market share leaders. While supplier diversification can strengthen
long-term supply security, we believe the ability to sustain yields and deliver
consistent product quality over time remains a key consideration in supplier

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

文曄(3036) 
26Q4資料中心恢復成長  
投資評等 目標價 
逢低買進 250 
前次投資建議 2026.8.6： 
「逢低買進」，目標價：$300

報告日期：2026.11.6 研 究 員：林秀泓

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 26Q4 資料中心產品恢復強勁季增，通訊、資料中心產品受益 AI需求。 
➢ 2026 營收翻倍，2027營收增速減緩。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/11/5 收盤價： 195.00 ➢ 26Q3 營收 5,973 億元，QoQ+1.1%，YoY+81.6%，符合
財測 QoQ-3.2%~+3.6%。毛利率 3.60%(QoQ+0.19pt，
YoY-0.14pt)，符合財測 3.5%~3.6%，主要因為產品組合
改善。EPS 7.97元。 
➢ 26Q4 文曄財測以美元匯率 32.2 估計，預估營收
7,300~7,770 億元(QoQ+22.2%~+30.1%)，主要由資料中
心出貨恢復帶動。毛利率 3.2%~3.3%。營業費用
7,154~7,546 億元。財測 EPS 8.07~9.05 元。 
➢ 展望 26Q4 各應用別(以美元估計)，汽車電子受提前拉貨
影響，預期季減雙位數百分比；工業與儀器因季節性因
素，預期季減中個位數百分比；通訊受益於 AI 光通訊需
求，預期季增高雙位數百分比；資料中心與伺服器預期季
增高雙位數百分比，全年成長約兩倍；消費性產品因提前
拉貨，預期季減中雙位數百分比；PC 預期季減；手機旺
季提前拉貨後，預期季減低雙位數百分比。 
➢ 調整預估 2026/2027 年營收 24,151 億元/27,170 億元，
YoY+105.0%/+12.5%，EPS 28.85 元/33.57 元。 
➢ 目前股價處於 6X 27(F) PER，為過去 PE區間 7~40X的
低位。考量 2026 年 AI 帶動營收大幅成長，2027 年 YoY
增速明顯減緩，投資評等維持「逢低買進」 ，調整目標價
250 元(7X 27(F) PER)，潛在上漲空間 28%。

營 運 分 析

文曄為 IC通路代理商 
文曄成立於 1993/12 成立，2002/08 上市掛牌，為提供全球專業電子零組件
通路服務的廠商，透過合併多家 IC通路商的方式持續擴大公司規模。營運總
部設立於台灣，目前在中國、韓國、新加坡、印度、泰國、馬來西亞、越南
等地區共有五十多個營運據點。代理半導體原廠超過 400 家，服務客戶超過
25,000 家。根據 2025 年 Gartner 最新數據，文曄已經成為全球最大和亞太
區最大的半導體通路商，市佔分別為 15.1%和 17.0%。 
產品組合方面，以代理產品的應用劃分營收，26Q3 汽車電子佔 5%、工業與
儀器佔 9%、通訊佔 13%、資料中心及伺服器佔 56%、消費性及其他佔 5%、
個人電腦與週邊佔 4%、手機佔 9%。

26Q3 財報符合財測，獲利再創單季新高 
26Q3 營收 5,973 億元，QoQ+1.1%，YoY+81.6%，符合財測 5,720~6,120
億元(QoQ-3.2%~+3.6%)。毛利率 3.60%(QoQ+0.19pt，YoY-0.14pt)，符合
財測 3.5%~3.6%，主要因為高毛利的工業、車用、Future 營收季增，產品組
合改善。營業費用QoQ+1.6%，費用率1.22%維持低檔，營業槓桿效益持續。
營業利益 142.3 億元，QoQ+9.5%，YoY+151.5%，優於財測中位數 140 億
元。業外方面，淨財務成本約19.4 億元，高於原先預估，因為資金成本上升。
其他業外收入 5.8 億元主要來自權益法認列日電貿的獲利，以及股利收入。
淨利 103.0 億元，QoQ+6.0%，YoY+169.9%，EPS 7.97元。

26Q4 營收預估季增，資料中心恢復成長 
26Q4 文曄財測以美元匯率 32.2 估計，預估營收 7,300~7,770 億元
(QoQ+22.2%~+30.1%，YoY+113.4%~+127.1%)，主要由資料中心出貨恢復
帶動 。 毛利率3.2%~3.3%。 營業費用7,154~7,546億元 。 財測EPS 8.07~9.05
元。

26Q4 資料中心恢復高雙位數季增，通訊持續成長 
26Q3 分產品線來看(以新台幣計)，汽車電子 QoQ+18%，優於前次法說預期
季減中低個位數百分比；工業與儀器 QoQ+13%，優於預期季增個位數百分
比；通訊 QoQ+5%，符合預期；資料中心與伺服器 QoQ-5%、YoY+114%，
符合預期；消費性產品 QoQ-7%，符合預期；PC週邊 QoQ持平，優於預期
季減個位數百分比，若不含記憶體則QoQ-11%、YoY-20%；手機QoQ+32%，
符合預期，受惠旺季與提前拉貨。 
展望 26Q4 各應用別(以美元估計)，汽車電子：受提前拉貨及庫存調整影響，
預期季減雙位數百分比，YoY+20%以上，全年YoY+30%以上；工業與儀器：
季節性因素，預期季減中個位數百分比，YoY+40%以上，全年成長約40%；
通訊：受益於 AI光通訊需求，預期季增高雙位數百分比，全年成長約一倍；
資料中心與伺服器：出貨節奏調整後恢復，預期季增高雙位數百分比，全年
成長約兩倍，2027 年預期維持高速成長；消費性產品：因提前拉貨，預期季
減中雙位數百分比；PC：預期季減；手機：旺季提前拉貨後，預期季減低雙
位數百分比。

半導體市場成長再度上修 
CSP 的 AI 資本支出展望正向，AI 需求強且具結構性。根據 Gartner 數據，
2026 年全球半導體市場由原先預估 YoY+92%再度上修到 YoY+108%，2027
年預估 YoY+39.2%(不含記憶體+33%)。半導體市場 2025~2029 年 CAGR
由 25.9%上修到 35.1%，2029 年市場規模預估達 2.7 兆美元。data center
為成長最強勁的領域，2026/2027 年預估 YoY+171%/+60%，2025~2029 年
CAGR+54.9%。 
文曄 26Q3 資料中心受出貨節奏影響，26Q4 恢復強勁季增，2026 全年成長
約兩倍，2027 年預期維持高速成長；通訊受益於 AI 光通訊需求，成長動能
延續。Future 方面，歐美市場持續正向復甦，26Q3 營收 YoY+40%以上，車
用與歐洲工廠自動化已無去庫存壓力，資料中心業務也開始接單出貨。Future
較高毛利率可部分抵銷 AI 產品線低毛利率的影響。調整預估 2026/2027 年
營收 24,151 億元/27,170 億元，YoY+105.0%/+12.5%，淨利 387 億元/450
億元，YoY+185.9%/+16.3%，EPS 28.85 元/33.57 元。

目前股價處於 6X 27(F) PER，為過去 PE區間 7~40X的低位。考量 2026 年
AI 帶動營收大幅成長，2027 年 YoY 增速明顯減緩，投資評等維持「逢低買
進」 ，調整目標價 250 元(7X 27(F) PER)，潛在上漲空間 28%。

### Page 1

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test  
78 
삼성전자  (005930) 
2026.11.04

STRESS TEST 
[반도체/전기전자] 고의영 
2122-9179 ey.ko@imfnsec.com

목표주가 37만원, ‘28년 기준으로는 56만원까지 열림 
투자의견 Buy, 목표주가 37만원으로 커버리지를 개시한다. ‘27 년 BPS에 목표 
P/B 2.3 배를 적용했다. 이는 ‘00년 이후 25년간 평균 P/B의 +2표준편차에 
해당한다[표1]. ‘26~’28년 ROE는 각각 56%, 5 9%, 4 8%로 지난 25년 평균인 
22%를 크게 상회할 전망이며, 고원형 ROE가 장기간 유지될 것으로 판단한다.  
이익 지속성에 대한 믿음이 공고해질 경우 시장의 시야는 ‘28년으로 향할 것이다. 
이 경우 ‘28년 BPS 기준 목표주가는 56만원까지 열릴 수 있다[표2]. 
동사 주가는 ‘26년 6월 고점 대비 10월 저점 기준 43% 하락해 과거 다운사이클 
평균 하락폭인 -41%를 넘어섰다. 특히 과거 8~13개월에 걸쳐 진행됐던 조정이 
이번에는 4개월 만에 나타났다. 시장이 ‘28년 이익 급감을 상정하고 있는 것이다. 
이러한 우려가 지속되는 한, 이익의 눈높이가 높아질수록 멀티플은 저렴해질 
뿐이며, DRAM 가격이 예상보다 더 올라갈 것이라는 소식이 주가의 촉매로 
작용하지 못한다. 결국 주가 반등을 위해서는 시장이 상정하고 있는 다운사이클이 
우려만큼 깊지 않을 것이라는 믿음이 필요한 것으로 본다.

STRESS TEST와 HBM 재평가 
① 당사는 고객들이 어느 정도 가격대에서 LTA를 해지하려는 유인이 생길지 
추정해봤다. Micron의 코멘트를 차용하여 ‘22~’23년 수준의 스트레스 테스트를 
시나리오 분석한 결과, 4Q27 고점 대비 DRAM 가격이 60% 수준 빠져야 해지가 
검토될 수 있다[그림2]. 그마저도 다음 공급 부족기의 물량 배정 우선순위 상실 
등 정성적 요인까지 감안하면 실제 해지 가능성은 더욱 낮은 것으로 판단한다. 
② HBM 비중이 높아질수록 동사의 이익 변동성은 낮아질 것이다. 특히 
중장기적으로 확대될 cHBM은 고객별 수주생산 제품으로 현물시장과 유통 
재고가 없고, 생산에 앞서 물량과 가격이 결정된다. 수요가 둔화되더라도 범용 
메모리처럼 재고 누적과 가격 급락으로 이어지기보다는 발주 조정이나 계약 
재협상을 통해 영향을 받는 구조다.  
동사 HBM 매출은 ‘26년 28조원에서 ‘28년 215조원으로 증가할 전망이다. 
‘27년 HBM 가격 방향성의 윤곽이 잡힌 현 시점에서, 동사 HBM 매출의 변수는 
가격이 아니라 물량이며, 물량은 고객과 사전에 협의되는 구조다. 과거 메모리 
업황에서는 2년 뒤의 이익을 가늠하기 어려웠으나, 이제는 HBM을 통해 중장기 
실적을 논의할 수 있게 됐다는 점, 그 자체에 주목한다. 
만약 동사의 ‘26~’28년 HBM 영업이익에 시기별 TSMC P/E (21배, 15배, 12배)를 
적용하면 HBM 가치는 동사 시가총액의 19%, 69%, 108%를 설명한다[표3]. 
특히 cHBM이 본격화할 ‘28년에는 HBM만으로 현재 시가총액이 설명될 수 
있다는 의미다. 시장이 통상 받아들이는 10배를 타겟팅해도 결과는 크게 다르지 
않다.

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test

‘27년 주가 경로: 회복, 박스권, 재평가 
연말까지는 급락 이후 저점을 높여가는 회복 구간일 전망이다.  
‘27년 상반기까지는 ‘28년 업황 지속성에 대한 우려가 상존할 전망이다. 이 불확실성이 
멀티플을 누르는 동안 ‘27년 이익은 계속 상향되므로 EPS 상승과 멀티플 하락이 상쇄되는 
구간이 이어질 전망이다. 기회비용은 결국 환원수익률로 보상받아야 하므로, ‘27년 1월 신규 
주주환원 정책이 중요한 변곡점이 될 것으로 판단한다.  
당사는 동사가 ‘27~’29년 FCF의 50%를 주주에게 환원하고, 이 중 70%를 배당, 30%를 
자사주 매입/소각으로 쓰는 것으로 가정하여, 현재 주가 기준 ‘27년 15% 수준의 
주주환원수익률을 기대한다. 미국 10년물 국채 금리가 5% 안팎인 환경에서 무위험 금리의 
3배에 달하는 환원수익률, 그중 배당만으로 10% 수준의 수익률은 그 자체로 희소한 투자 
포인트로 볼 수 있다. 
‘27년 하반기 이후에는, 실적 추정의 기준 연도가 ‘28년으로 넘어가고, 이후 신규 팹 물량이 
소량 출하되면서 센티먼트상 우려는 커질 것이다. 그러나 LTA가 이익을 지킨다는 사실이 
숫자로 확인되고 cHBM의 가치가 재평가된다면 순환 할인이 축소될 것으로 판단한다.

업사이드 요인 
① AI 투자 지속성 우려로 최근 주가 하락이 가팔랐던 만큼, 이 부분에 대한 우려가 
완화되면 반등의 속도와 폭이 예상보다 빠를 수 있다 
② 향후 주주환원 규모가 예상보다 더 적극적일 경우 추가적인 업사이드가 될 수 있다. 
혹은 주주환원 이후에도 남는 대규모 FCF의 활용 방안도 촉매가 될 수 있다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

종목추천 투자등급

주가 추이

3분기 영업 이 익 은 지상방산 매출 감소와 일 회성 비용 등의 영 향 으로 
기대치를 대폭 하회했습니다. 4분기 실적 회복 과 중 장기 실적 성장 대
비 저평가된 밸 류 에이 션 에 주목합 니 다. 
3Q26 Review: 영업이익 0.5조원으로 컨센서스 대폭 하회 
3분기 매출액은 6.1조원으로 전년대비 6.0% 감소했다. 항공우주 부문
과 연결자회사들 성장에도 불구하고 지상방산 매출이 전년대비 약 0.9
조원 감소한 영향이다. 지상방산 내수와 수출 매출은 각각 0.7조원, 0.6
조원으로 전년대비 28.6%, 50.4% 감소했다. 내수는 대전 사업장 생산 
중단, 수출은 4분기 납기 집중에 기인한다. 항공우주 매출은 군수사업 
물량 증가로 전년대비 9.3% 증가한 0.7조원을 기록했다. 연결 자회사
인 한화오션과 한화시스템 매출은 각각 3.4조원, 0.8조원으로 전년대비 
12.9%, 30.1% 증가했다. 영업이익은 0.5조원으로 전년대비 49.2% 감
소했으며 영업이익률은 7.4%로 6.3%P 하락했다. 한화오션과 한화시스
템의 이익 증가에도 지상방산 이익 감소와 필리조선소 적자 반영이 영
향을 미쳤다. 지상방산은 일회성 비용 약 460억원, 원화 강세, 매출 감
소에 따른 고정비 부담 증가가 복합적으로 작용했다. 세전이익은 한화
오션의 외화환산손실 영향으로 -0.2조원을 기록하며 적자전환했다.  
실적 회복과 저평가 매력에 주목할 시점 
4분기 영업이익은 1.7조원으로 추정하며 시장 기대치를 상회할 전망이
다. 지상방산 수출 인도 물량이 집중되는 영향이다. 2027년 예상 실적 
기준 P/E는 12.5배로 경쟁사 Rheinmetall(22.5배) 대비 낮다. 약 35조
원의 방산 수주잔고를 기반으로 최소 2029년까지 실적 성장이 예상되
는 만큼 저평가 매력이 높다. 신규 수주 시점과 실적에 대한 우려가 상
존하지만 현재 밸류에이션에는 상당 부분 반영된 것으로 판단한다. 미
국 중간선거 등 국방예산 관련 정책 불확실성과 환율 부담이 완화되면 
투자자들의 관심은 4분기 실적과 2027년 신규 수주로 이동할 전망이
다. 연말 주요국의 국방예산 확정은 신규 수주 시점에 대한 불확실성을 
낮추는 계기가 될 수 있다.  
투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 1,300,000원 유지 
글로벌 재무장 수요 확대에 따른 동사의 중장기 성장 전망은 여전히 
유효하다. 투자의견 BUY와 목표주가 1,300,000원을 유지한다.

이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (채운샘) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
한화에어로스페이스는 당사와 ‘독점규제 및 공정거래에 관한 법률’ 에 따른 계열회사 관계에 있는 법인입니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 6

한화에어로스페이스 (012450) [한화리서치]

6 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 30 日(金)

1/5 
AI バブル測定器としての鉱工業生産（26 年９月） 
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

・筆者が日本経済と株式市場の行方を読む上で定点観測している日本の鉱工業生産は、2026年
９月に前月比＋2.2％となり、市場予想（同＋1.3％）を上回った。経産産業省の予測補正値
（同＋1.6％）に対しても上振れた。増産は３ヶ月ぶり。８月が速報段階の同▲1.7％から確
報で同▲2.4％へと下方修正されていたため、９月の増産で前月までの落ち込みを概ね取り戻
した格好となる。もっとも、生産水準は直近最高だった６月の104.6を上回るには至っておら
ず、数量ベースの生産活動は、経済産業省の判断通り「一進一退」となっている。

・日経平均株価は先行き12 ヶ月72,000円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPYは先行き12ヶ月 155円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を12月に 1.5％とした後、27年末までに2.25％とするだろう 
・FEDはFF金利を、年内は4.0％で据え置くだろう

### Page 2

2/5 
・増産となったのは８業種。上位３業種は自動車工業、生産用機械工業、汎用・業務機械工業
であった。反対に減産の寄与度が大きかった３業種は鉄鋼・非鉄金属、窯業・土石製品工
業、化学工業（除く無機・有機化学工業・医薬品）であった。７月に熊本で発生した地震に
よって８月までは自動車生産などに影響が出ていたが、その挽回生産が９月の数値を押し上
げる格好となった。

・10月初旬に実施された生産予測調査に基づけば、製造工業の生産計画は10月に前月比＋5.1％
となった後、11月は同＋0.8％と増産が見込まれている。経産省経済解析室が生産予測指数の
上振れバイアスを補正した10月の予測値は同＋3.9％とかなり強い。電子部品・デバイス工業
と生産用機械工業で大幅な増産が見込まれているほか、自動車の挽回生産も続く見込みであ
る。生産活動全体の方向感を決める輸送機械工業は９月（実績）に前月比＋5.5％となった
後、10月（計画）に同＋0.9％、11月に同＋4.3％となっている。日本や米国における自動車
販売台数が堅調に推移していることを踏まえると、基調的に弱含んでいく展開は想定しにく
い。また生産活動の先行指標として有用な製造業ＰＭＩが４月以降に有意な上昇を示してい
ることから判断すると、製造業全体としてはＡＩ・半導体需要が浸透する形で増勢が強まっ
ていくと期待される。10月製造業ＰＭＩは53.4と高水準を維持している。

・なお、鉱工業生産指数は生産の「数量」に重きを置いて生産高を計測する統計であり、「付
加価値」を直接捕捉する性格のものではない。例えば、高付加価値を狙った品質向上によっ
て生産数量・重量が減少する場合、統計上はそれを減産と見做し、付加価値を過小評価して
しまうこともある。したがって、鉱工業生産統計が示すほど日本経済（製造業）の付加価値

### Page 3

3/5 
創出力が停滞していない可能性を念頭に置く必要がある。事実、過去数年は付加価値の合計
である実質ＧＤＰと鉱工業生産指数の水準・方向感の乖離が大きくなっている。

・株式市場における重要度が高い電子部品・デバイス工業については、前月比＋3.6％の増産で
あった。地震の影響もあってか、ＩＣの増産に一服感がみられる一方、コンデンサや電子回
路基板などで増産の動きがみられる。生産用機械工業に分類される半導体製造装置は同＋
3.4％の増産となり、３ヶ月平均値でみても＋7.7％と力強い増産基調にある。生産予測調査
に目を向けると、電子部品・デバイス工業は10月に同＋16.6％、11月に同▲1.7％と均してみ
れば大幅な増産が計画されている。半導体製造装置が含まれる生産用機械工業は10月に同＋
15.5％と大幅増産となった後、11月は同▲5.7％と減産が見込まれているが、こちらも基調は
強い。個別企業の業績予想などから判断しても、先行きは半導体製造装置の増産がかなりの
高確度で期待できる。関連指標の機械受注統計における「電子計算機等」の受注額水準も鈍
化する気配に乏しい。この指標は日経平均株価との連動性がある。

### Page 4

4/5

・電子部品・デバイス工業の動向を仔細にみると、９月は出荷が前年比＋0.8％へと減速するな
か、在庫は同▲6.1％へとマイナス幅を拡大した。この結果、出荷・在庫バランス（両者の前
年比差分から算出）は＋6.9pとなり、３ヶ月平均でも＋7.0ptとはっきりとしたプラス領域を
維持した。引き続き、製品需給が引き締まる方向にあることが示唆されている。ここで電子
部品・デバイス工業の出荷・在庫バランスと、日経平均株価が長期的に連動性を有してきた
事実を再確認したい。株価指数において半導体を直接手掛ける企業の存在感は必ずしも大き
くはないものの、半導体製造装置、電子部品、化学品など「広義半導体」で見ればその存在
感は大きく、結果的に日本株全体のうねりを作り出す構図があると筆者は理解している。ち
なみに出荷在庫バランス株価は方向感こそ一致しているものの、両者のかい離は大きくなっ
ている。その要因として、数量ベースの出荷・在庫バランスには、メモリなど製品価格の高
騰が反映されないことが考えられる。

る。過去の経験則に従えば、今後は右上領域（在庫増・出荷増）に向けて歩み出すと予想さ
れる。

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX tăng điểm trong tuần qua, +68.24 điểm tương ứng +4.00%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Thiết bị điện, Khách sạn - Nhà hàng và Thiết bị dịch vụ y tế là các nhóm ngành có 
mức tăng rõ rệt nhất trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần 
trước với giá trị -966 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã FPT, ACB và VCB; mặt khác, tự doanh duy 
trì mua ròng ở mức 137 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều mua ròng 1,335 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua nhưng đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -3,049 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng 
1,815 hợp đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -6,000 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam hồi phục trở lại sau bốn tuần giảm liên tiếp, khi VN-Index bật tăng mạnh 
từ vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1.700 điểm với thanh khoản cải thiện. Sau nhịp điều chỉnh kéo dài 
khiến mặt bằng định giá về vùng hấp dẫn, lực cầu bắt đáy đã nhập cuộc quyết liệt hơn, 
với phiên 21/10 tăng gần 47 điểm — một trong những phiên tăng mạnh nhất nhiều tháng 
gần đây. Điểm tích cực và đáng chú ý nhất là khối ngoại đã xuất hiện hai phiên đảo chiều 
mua ròng trở lại sau khoảng 20 phiên bán ròng liên tiếp, tập trung vào nhóm Bluechip 
như VIC, TCB, VHM, MSN — tín hiệu đầu tiên cho thấy áp lực rút vốn ngoại có thể đang dần 
hạ nhiệt. Thông tin tích cực tác động lên thị trường đến từ các chỉ đạo của NHNN và Chính 
phủ về việc hạ mặt bằng lãi suất để hỗ trợ tăng trưởng kinh tế. Bên cạnh đó, mùa báo cáo 
KQKD quý 3 khả quan đang diễn ra cũng là yếu tố hỗ trợ xu hướng thị trường chung.

Trung tâm phân tích 
(+84) 24-7303-5333  kbsv.research@kbsec.com.vn

19/10/2026

Nguồn: V_KBSource

Triển vọng kinh tế 4Q2026 
Giữ nhịp tăng trưởng, vững vàng về itch

Một số dự báo chính của KBSV về kinh tế Việt Nam trong năm 2026:

1) Tăng trưởng GDP năm 2026 được dự báo sẽ đạt 9.0%, với động lực chính 
đến từ định hướng đẩy mạnh giải ngân đầu tư công của Chính phủ, dòng 
vốn FDI duy trì tích cực và hoạt động sản xuất công nghiệp phục hồi theo 
xuất khẩu. Về rủi ro, chúng tôi cho rằng Việt Nam sẽ tiếp tục đối mặt với 
một số yếu tố bất định từ bên ngoài, bao gồm: rủi ro xung đột Mỹ – Iran 
kéo dài hơn dự kiến, xu hướng tăng của mặt bằng lãi suất toàn cầu gây gia 
tăng áp lực tài chính và kéo giảm tổng cầu, cùng nguy cơ vỡ bong bóng AI.

2) Chúng tôi nâng dự báo lạm phát bình quân năm 2026 của Việt Nam lên 
mức 4.7% YoY, cao hơn mục tiêu kiểm soát lạm phát của Chính phủ, dựa 
trên kịch bản dự báo thận trọng hơn về diễn biến xung đột địa chính trị và 
giá năng lượng thế giới trong ngắn hạn.

3) Mặt bằng lãi suất trong quý IV được dự báo sẽ ổn định quanh vùng hiện 
tại và không xuất hiện thêm nhịp tăng đột biến, nhờ dòng tiền cải thiện 
từ giải ngân đầu tư công và kỳ vọng có thêm các biện pháp hỗ trợ thanh 
khoản trung dài hạn từ phía Chính phủ và NHNN.

4) Tỷ giá USD/VND trong quý cuối năm có thể chịu một số sức ép do Fed dự 
báo còn thêm một lần tăng lãi suất nữa trong năm. Dù vậy, chúng tôi cho 
rằng tỷ giá USD/VND khó tăng mạnh mà chỉ quay lại mức đầu năm, dựa 
trên kỳ vọng chỉ số DXY không biến động mạnh, nguồn cung ngoại tệ cải 
thiện (FDI giải ngân, kiều hối, nâng hạng TTCK) và chênh lệch lãi suất 
VND–USD duy trì trạng thái dương.

Tham khảo nội dung chi tiết trong báo cáo tại đây.

21/10/2026

CTCP VÀNG BẠC ĐÁ QUÝ PHÚ NHUẬN (PNJ)  
Cập nhật ĐHĐCĐ bất thường 21/10/2026

Tái cơ cấu để xử lý áp lực thanh khoản  
— ĐHĐCĐ bất thường PNJ ngày 21/10/2026 đã thông qua điều chỉnh kế hoạch kinh 
doanh và phương án phát hành riêng lẻ để bổ sung vốn tái cấu trúc. Ban lãnh đạo đặt 
ra các vấn đề trọng yếu là đáp ứng nhu cầu thanh khoản và chuẩn bị nguồn lực để thực 
hiện các cam kết thu đổi đã tích lũy trong quá khứ. Bên cạnh đó, các cổ đông tham dự 
cũng đặt các câu hỏi liên quan đến tình hình hoạt động qua các điểm chính (1) dòng 
tiền hoạt động kinh doanh; (2) diễn biến thị trường sau khi PNJ áp dụng chính sách thu 
đổi mới; (3) hiệu quả hoạt động sau tái cấu trúc.

Nội dung chính tại đại hội 
— Theo Ban lãnh đạo, thông tin liên quan đến P-Lab từ đầu tháng 7 khiến khách hàng gia 
tăng bán lại sản phẩm. Tổng giá trị thu đổi và mua lại trong tháng đạt gần 6,000 tỷ VND, 
so với khoảng 7,000 tỷ VND trong cả năm 2025. Theo BCTC riêng, nguồn tiền khả dụng 
giảm từ khoảng 3,700 tỷ VND cuối Q2/2026 xuống 770 tỷ VND cuối Q3/2026, đã bao 
gồm khoản vay 700 tỷ VND từ Chủ tịch HĐQT. Tỷ trọng kim cương và trang sức kim 
cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% lên 52%. 
— Q3/2026 ghi nhận lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND trước phần tăng trích lập dự phòng. Để giảm 
áp lực thanh toán, PNJ kéo dài thời gian chi trả đối với kim cương lên 120 ngày từ tháng 
07/2026. Thay đổi chính sách cũng ảnh hưởng đến niềm tin khách hàng và sức mua, cụ 
thể doanh thu và lợi nhuận gộp Q3/2026 đạt lần lượt 6,995/609 tỷ VND, giảm 14%/62% 
YoY. Chi phí bán hàng và quản lý giảm 13% YoY chưa đủ bù đắp mức giảm lợi nhuận 
gộp, khiến PNJ lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND, so với lãi 496 tỷ VND trong Q3/2025. 
— PNJ nâng giả định tỷ lệ thu đổi trên giá trị thu đổi còn lại lên 80%, so với 28.8% đối với 
nhóm sản phẩm giai đoạn 2020–2026 trong ước tính tại BCTC bán niên 2026. Tổng dự 
phòng tổn thất dự kiến tăng từ 2,267 tỷ VND lên 7,071 tỷ VND. Kế hoạch điều chỉnh ghi 
nhận doanh thu 39,057 tỷ VND, lỗ trước thuế 6,059 tỷ VND và lỗ sau thuế 6,271 tỷ VND. 
Theo Ban lãnh đạo, LNST loại trừ dự phòng ước khoảng 800 tỷ VND, chủ yếu nhờ kết 
quả Q1/2026. Khoản dự phòng phản ánh tổn thất ước tính cho toàn bộ kịch bản, không 
đồng nghĩa toàn bộ giá trị sẽ được chi trả ngay trong kỳ. 
— Giá trị cam kết thu đổi tích lũy đối với kim cương và trang sức kim cương tại cuối 
Q3/2026 được PNJ ước tính khoảng 21,000 tỷ VND. Với hàng đã bán, doanh nghiệp dự 
kiến chuyển đổi quyền lợi khách hàng sang sản phẩm và các chương trình ưu đãi. Với 
sản phẩm bán mới, chính sách thu đổi định hướng theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm 
giao dịch, thay cho tỷ lệ cố định trên giá trị hóa đơn, nhằm hạn chế tiếp tục tích lũy 
nghĩa vụ tiềm tàng trong tương lai theo cơ chế cũ. 
Tham khảo nội dung chi tiết trong báo cáo tại đây.

Quan điểm kỹ thuật 
Xu hướng & Hành động

Chỉ số VNINDEX

Source: Trading View, KB Securities Vietnam

Ngắn hạn 
(Dưới 3 tháng)

Trung hạn (Từ 3 đến 9 tháng)

Chỉ báo  Nhận định xu hướng Hành động 
Xanh đậm  Khả năng tăng điểm cao Mua mạnh 
Xanh nhạt  Khả năng tăng điểm vừa phải Mua từng phần 
Vàng  Xu hướng trung tính Mua/Bán trading 
Cam  Khả năng giảm điểm vừa phải Bán từng phần 
Đỏ  Khả năng giảm điểm cao Bán mạnh

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành cây nến xanh thân dài trên khung tuần và đáng chú ý là cho phản ứng 
bật tăng ngay tại cạnh dưới của mẫu hình tam giác đi ngang trung hạn, cho thấy tín hiệu 
tích cực đã quay trở lại với xu hướng ngắn hạn. Trên khung ngày, sau 2 phiên điều chỉnh, 
rung lắc vào đầu tuần, chỉ số cũng đảo chiều tăng điểm khá dốc, với 3 cây nến xanh liên 
tiếp với thanh khoản được tăng cường, củng cố thêm tín hiệu xác nhận cho đà hồi phục. 
Sau nhịp bật tăng mạnh từ đáy, VNINDEX được dự báo sẽ sớm gặp phải áp lực điều chỉnh 
khi quay lên điểm giữa của dải đi ngang theo mẫu hình tam giác, tương ứng vùng kháng cự 
kế tiếp tại quanh 1810. Tuy nhiên, xu hướng ngắn hạn vẫn có phần nghiêng về chiều tăng, 
với tín hiệu xác nhận đáy của một số mã cổ phiếu sau quá trình sụt giảm kéo dài.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1850 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1810 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1750 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1710 (+-15)

### Page 6

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

trưởng 6%-8%/năm

### Page 2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -32.53 điểm tương ứng -1.83%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Linh kiện ô tô, Dầu khí và Công nghệ là các nhóm ngành có m ức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -634 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã FPT, ACB và VPL; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng ở mức 
-609 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -5,829 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -1,462 hợp đồng. Tự doanh duy trì mua ròng 722 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -5,278 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam đánh mất đà hồi phục của tuần trước, khi VN-Index quay đầu giảm khoảng 
1,8% về đóng cửa quanh 1.742 điểm, với phiên cuối tuần có thời điểm lùi sâu về sát 1.727 
điểm trước khi thu hẹp đà giảm. Dù mùa báo cáo KQKD quý 3 diễn ra tương đối tích cực, 
tuy nhiên các lo ngại về việc môi trường lãi suất cao vẫn duy trì trong bối cảnh căng thẳng 
Trung Đông leo thang đang đè nặng lên tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Kết quả kỳ họp của FED trong 
tuần qua nhìn chung phù hợp với kỳ vọng và không gây ra ảnh hưởng đáng kể. Điểm kém 
tích cực nhất là khối ngoại đã quay lại bán ròng với tổng giá trị khoảng 2.700 tỷ đồng 
trong tuần — đảo ngược diễn biến mua ròng cuối tuần trước, cho thấy đà rút vốn ngoại 
vẫn chưa thực sự dừng lại.

Chỉ số VNINDEX

Source: Trading View, KB Securities Vietnam

Ngắn hạn 
(Dưới 3 tháng)

Trung hạn (Từ 3 đến 9 tháng)

Chỉ báo  Nhận định xu hướng Hành động 
Xanh đậm  Khả năng tăng điểm cao Mua mạnh 
Xanh nhạt  Khả năng tăng điểm vừa phải Mua từng phần 
Vàng  Xu hướng trung tính Mua/Bán trading

Đỏ  Khả năng giảm điểm cao Bán mạnh

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tuần qua hình thành mẫu nến giảm trên đồ thị tuần, nhưng chưa thoái lui quá 1 
nửa cây nến tăng của tuần trước, cùng thanh khoản được tiết giảm, hàm ý nhịp điều chỉnh 
vẫn trong phạm vi cho phép. Trên khung ngày, sau bốn phiên giằng co đầu tuần với thanh 
khoản thấp, chỉ số đã chịu áp lực sụt giảm mạnh hơn trong phiên cuối tuần, chớm xuống 
dưới đường tín hiệu chính giữa dải Bollinger, nhưng cho tín hiệu rút chân ngay sau đó, thể 
hiện áp lực bán tháo đã tạm thời được kiểm soát. Mặc dù rủi ro mở rộng thêm nhịp điều 
chỉnh vẫn còn để ngỏ, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn đang được bảo lưu, VNINDEX 
được kỳ vọng sẽ cho phản ứng hồi phục trở lại tại các điểm đỡ bên dưới.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1850 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1800 (+-10)

lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng.

trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

giá khuyến nghị mua.

Document 316: Cliff Notes_ the source and persistence of inflation (2026-11-12)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ the source and persistence of inflation (2026-11-12).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 11
Status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: the source and persistence of inflation
By Ryan Wells, Illiana Jain & Elliot Clarke
11:16 October 30 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia this week, all eyes were on the September CPI. Headline inflation rose 0.8% (4.3%yr) in
the month, largely owing to a 9.4% jump in fuel prices and a seasonal surge in domestic and
international holiday travel & accommodation. The underlying inflation pulse also proved hotter than

anticipated for Q3 overall, up 1.0% (3.7%yr).

### Page 2

While some parts of the basket are seeing disinflation – housing in particular looks to be cooling faster
than expected as builders lure sales with discounts – other components are still showing persistence.
Highlighting this is market services ex volatiles, which the RBA watches closely as an indicator of price
pressures related to domestic capacity. In Q3, this metric rose 1.2% (3.2%yr), and the monthly detail
points to momentum holding up – in line with our expectations for another chunky read in Q4.

With inflation running well above the RBA’s August forecasts, and some of the energy risks flagged
then materialising, we continue to expect the Monetary Policy Board will deliver a 25bp rate hike to
4.85% in November. Beyond November, another rate hike in February is a risk, but is not currently part
of our base case.
Across the Tasman, the RBNZ this week surprised market expectations for a 25bp rate hike, deciding
instead to wait for further information before taking the next step with policy. The decision was made

4:3, with the Governor in the ‘on hold’ camp. A 25bp hike in December to 3.00% and further tightening
in 2027 (Westpac NZ forecasts a peak of 4.00% in September) seems probable; however, each
decision will depend on fuel prices, inflation expectations and evidence of second-round pricing
pressures, along with the evolution of global economic risks.
In the US, the FOMC also held rates steady at its October meeting. Based on prior comments by key
members, the decision was not a surprise, and neither were Governor Cook and Cleveland Fed

President Hammack’s dissents in favour of a 25bp hike. The Committee remains positive on economic
activity, characterising it in the statement as “expanding at a strong pace” (an upgrade from “solid” in
September). Chair Warsh also subsequently emphasised the breadth of economic growth outside of
tech-related investment (evinced by Q3 GDP the day after; see below).
The Committee refrained from spotlighting key price risks in the statement. However, inflation “remains

elevated” and the FOMC are determined to achieve a “timely” and sustainable return of annual inflation
to 2.0%yr after 6 years above target. This guidance and the “accumulation of more information will help
the Committee determine the policy stance” suggests another 25bp hike is likely in December.
Thereafter, the FOMC will remain attentive to any broadening in inflation and related risks through
2027; although, in the absence of another shock, we expect them to remain on hold.

The Q3 GDP report certainly supported Chair Warsh’s view on the breadth of growth, the 3.4%
annualised gain for GDP benefitting from a 3.8% annualised increase in household consumption,
12.7% growth in non-residential investment as well as a robust increase in government spending.
Notably though, the contribution from business investment was effectively offset by net exports’ drag
given most of the componentry related to AI infrastructure investment is imported. This is not to say that

US firms won’t benefit from the investment once complete, but simply that much of the initial return
goes to the foreign businesses who provide the necessary capital equipment.
On consumption, the monthly PCE data implies downside risks remain for the consumer, the increase
in nominal spending seen in the month requiring dissaving and, on a real basis, consumption flat in the
month following a 0.5% gain in August. Cost-of-living pressures and uncertainty over the outlook is

### Page 3

Related articles
expected to see household consumption growth average around trend over the coming year, albeit with
considerable volatility quarter to quarter. This should see PCE inflation slow back towards its 2.0%yr
medium-term target as the FOMC desires, from 3.6%yr and 3.1%yr for the headline and core
measures respectively as of September.
Across the Atlantic, the ECB also left its key policy rates unchanged in October. Of note from the
meeting communications, energy prices continue to pose upside risks for inflation, but also downside

risks for growth. "While the full effects of the energy shock are still unfolding, underlying inflation has
remained broadly stable so far and wages do not show a material response". "Financing conditions
have [also] tightened", "which is likely to weigh on inflation and growth". In the press conference,
President Lagarde noted that, between the October and December meetings, a significant amount of
new economic information will be released, allowing forecasts and risks assessments to be tested.

Based on the October meeting communications, we continue to expect a rate hike at the December
meeting but believe this will be prove to be the last hike of the cycle. Note though, the spectrum of risks
Europe faces is broad and skewed towards inflation in the near term, hence the Council’s guidance will
remain hawkish. President Lagarde also announced today that she will step down as ECB President in
February ahead of the scheduled conclusion of her term next October.

### Page 4

Monthly Economics
Sector Analysis
Personal Consumption Expenditures (PCE)
• We've lifted our Q3 expectation for real PCE on incoming data and a slightly firmer consumer
backdrop in the wake of recent revisions.
Consumer spending looks firmer in the third quarter, and we now expect real PCE to rise at a 3.4%
annualized clip. This upward revision reflects recent data, with August consumption coming in firm
with real spending rising by the fastest monthly clip in nearly two years, but also reflects a slightly

firmer consumer backdrop in the wake of the BEA's annual revisions released late September. Although
revisions did not materially alter spending, they lifted the level of household income in recent years,
which also boosted the personal saving rate. The trajectory of real income growth remains weak, but
the consumer still looks to be in a slightly stronger financial position than previously believed, which
helps explain recent resilience in spending and suggests consumers may have a bit more runway ahead.

Investment: Equipment, Intellectual Property Products and Inventories
• The AI build out will lead to another surge in business fixed investment spending in Q3, though the
effect on headline growth will be partly offset by stronger imports.
Business fixed investment spending continues to be supported primarily by the high-tech buildout.
We forecast real equipment investment to rise at around a 16% annualized clip in Q3, but part of
this boost to headline GDP growth will be offset by stronger import growth that is facilitating the

build out. Net imports of computers are tracking to surge in the third quarter, which will provide a
sizable lift to information processing equipment investment, or around half of overall equipment.
And while non-defense capital goods shipments (including aircraft) slipped in August, the near-term
growth trajectory remains supportive of a pickup in equipment. The rebuild in inventories we have
been anticipating is also tracking to be a bit stronger as businesses replenish drawn down product.

Investment: Residential
• The abrupt rise in mortgage rates is likely to dampen residential investment over the next few
quarters. Residential spending should find support next year as longer-term interest rates
moderate somewhat.
We are unlikely to see another positive residential investment reading in Q3. Rising mortgage rates

have sent a chill over builder outlooks, pushing sales expectations to their weakest level in more than
three years. While builders have responded with deeper price cuts, affordability pressures continue
to weigh on single-family sales and construction outlays. Apartment construction is simultaneously
soft amid rising financing costs and modest rent growth, and home improvement spending remains
constrained by high materials costs and interest rates. While residential investment is likely to remain

4 | Economics

### Page 5

Economic Outlook: October 2026 Economics
a drag this year, we expect a slight moderation in longer-term interest rates in 2027, which would
provide a more supportive backdrop.
Investment: Nonresidential Structures
• We have not made material changes to our nonresidential structures forecast. Despite elevated
interest rates, data center and related spending should continue to support structures investment
through our forecast horizon.
Nonresidential structures spending has accelerated sharply, overwhelmingly driven by data centers

and electric power facilities. As data center construction expands geographically and demand rises
for semiconductors, high-tech capex has also started to generate positive spillovers for warehousing
and manufacturing construction. This buildout, which appears relatively insensitive to interest rates,
should be enough to sustain solid growth in structures investment even as spending across most
other categories remains essentially flat. Domestic energy is another green shoot, with elevated prices

supporting ongoing growth in active rotary rigs.

Net Exports & Trade
• An expected drag from trade in Q3 got larger in light of a near-record trade deficit in August, net
exports is now expected to lop off three full percentage points from third quarter growth.

Exports increased in August for the first time in four months, but the increase was more than offset by
an even larger increase in imports. The upshot was a widening in the trade deficit to a near-record gap
of more than $105 billion. Going into the report, we had a 1.9 percentage point drag from net exports
penciled in for Q3 GDP; we revised that to a full 3.0 percentage points drag in light of the record gap.
Even a modest narrowing in the final months of the year would likely mean a lift to headline GDP in

Q4, but the real story here is that trade will remain a headwind throughout the forecast period owing
largely to surging imports of tech-related products amid the AI boom.
Labor Market
• The jobs market remains stable. We continue to expect the unemployment rate to end the year at
4.2% and hold near that level in 2027.
We modestly revised down our near-term payroll growth forecast following the September jobs
report, but otherwise we have left our labor market outlook unchanged. Measures of labor demand

growth continue to point to a stable labor market. Job openings have inched higher on a year-ago
basis but remain broadly steady while initial jobless claims, WARN notices and Challenger job cut
announcements continue to signal low layoffs. Labor force participation appears to be improving after
its sharp pullback in the first half of the year. Looking ahead, we expect payroll growth to average
around 60K per month in 2027, roughly in line with our estimate of breakeven job growth. That should

Economics | 5

### Page 6

Monthly Economics
keep the unemployment rate near 4.2% in 2027 and leave labor market conditions broadly consistent
with "full employment." Notably, our expectation for labor cost growth of ~3.4% is the reason we
continue to reiterate that labor cost growth is not a contributor to the excess inflation seen in the
data.
Inflation
• We expect the core PCE deflator to rise 3.2% and 2.6% Q4/Q4 in 2026 and 2027, respectively.
Headline inflation remains heavily influenced by developments in the Middle East, with higher oil

prices lifting gasoline and diesel prices. Food inflation has been tame this year but looks poised to
strengthen as higher transportation and commodity prices work their way through supply chains.
Beyond food and energy, price pressures remain concentrated in a handful of categories, most notably
airfares and AI-related tech goods. Core goods inflation is showing signs of stabilizing, as waning tariff-
related price pressures have been offset by resilient demand tied to the AI buildout. We expect some

improvement here in 2027. Still-low shelter inflation and cooler price growth for airfares and health
care should help bring about some moderation in core services inflation next year. We expect the core
PCE deflator to rise 3.2% on a Q4-over-Q4 basis in 2026 before slowing to 2.6% in 2027.

Fiscal Policy
• Fiscal year (FY) 2026 ended on September 30, and the federal government tallied a $2.0 trillion
budget deficit (6.5% of GDP). We project the deficit to be unchanged in dollar terms in FY 2027

and a bit smaller as a share of GDP (~6%).
• Another continuing resolution to extend government funding past the December 11 deadline
seems like the most likely outcome at this time for the FY 2027 budget. It is possible Republicans
use the lame duck session of Congress to increase defense spending and raise the debt ceiling.
Looking ahead to the lame duck session of Congress that will follow the midterm elections, Congress
needs to pass a bill that extends government funding past December 11 to avert a government

shutdown. Another continuing resolution that punts the issue into Q1 seems like the most likely
outcome. Perhaps more interesting will be whether Republicans attempt to use budget reconciliation
to pass one more partisan piece of legislation. If they do, we suspect the two things at the top the
agenda will be more funding for national defense and immigration enforcement priorities as well as a
debt ceiling increase. For the latter, raising it in the lame duck would remove some leverage Democrats

could have in 2027 if they recapture one or both chambers. It is still very early, but we estimate the
debt ceiling "X date" by which Congress must act or face default is sometime in late Q3/early Q4 of
next year.
6 | Economics

### Page 7

Economic Outlook: October 2026 Economics
Monetary Policy & Interest Rates
• Our base case forecast is for the FOMC to hike the fed funds rate by 25 bps in December and
then keep the policy rate unchanged in 2027 at 4.00%-4.25%, with risks skewed toward additional
tightening.
As we go to print, the FOMC seems more likely than not to keep the fed funds rate unchanged in
October. But given the rationale under which the Fed started this tightening cycle and the forcefulness

with which Warsh has driven home his displeasure with elevated inflation, it seems entirely plausible
to us that October will be a live meeting. At a minimum, the recent inflation data seem to make a hike
in December a lock. Past December, the outlook will be highly dependent on future inflation data. Our
expectation is that core inflation will cool gradually in 2027, and we still do not see signs of demand-
side inflation in the data. But hiking cycles have an inertial component to them, and the recent inflation

data reset the clock to zero on the Fed seeing several good inflation prints in a row. This creates upside
risk to our base case for the fed funds rate.

International Developments
• Global growth remains resilient, leaving our 2026 and 2027 forecasts unchanged. Our inflation
forecasts are also unchanged, although risks of delayed price pass-through could prolong inflation
pressures into 2027.

• Monetary policy continues to lean toward tightening across advanced economies, while emerging
market central banks are increasingly cautious, either holding rates or easing more gradually as
inflation pressures rebuild.
The balance of risks to global inflation remains tilted to the upside, complicating the monetary policy
outlook even as growth has proven more resilient than expected. Elevated energy prices, particularly
for refined petroleum products, persistent geopolitical uncertainty and El Niño-related food price

risks could prolong inflationary pressures into 2027. While second-round effects still remain relatively
contained, repeated supply shocks are testing central banks’ willingness to look through the temporary
price increases, particularly where inflation expectations remain elevated, even as higher government
bond yields are already contributing to tighter financial conditions.
Across advanced economies, central banks face increasingly difficult trade-offs as persistent inflation

pressures limit the scope for policy accommodation. The Eurozone remains particularly vulnerable
to energy supply disruptions, with elevated natural gas prices and low storage levels adding to
inflation risks. We continue to expect a 25 bps European Central Bank hike in December, followed by a
prolonged hold through 2027. Fiscal concerns are also adding to pressure, particularly in France, where
the selloff in government debt has pushed yields higher. In the U.K., resilient activity and renewed

Economics | 7

### Page 8

Monthly Economics
inflation pressures similarly underpin our expectation for a Bank of England hike in November,
followed by a hold through 2027.
Elsewhere, policy paths are becoming more differentiated. After four hikes this year brought the Cash
Rate to 4.60%, we expect the Reserve Bank of Australia to remain on hold, although renewed inflation
pressures or stronger-than-expected activity could prompt further tightening. We also still expect
the Bank of Canada to hold at 2.25%, with subdued growth weighing against energy-driven inflation

risks, although incoming inflation data over the coming weeks will be critical in determining whether
persistent price pressures warrant tightening monetary policy perhaps as early as the December
meeting. Japan remains an exception to the broader hold outlook: while the yen's recovery provides
some relief from imported inflation, elevated energy prices and import dependence continue to pose
upside risks. We expect the Bank of Japan to hike again by year-end and now expect an additional 25

bps hike in Q3-2027, bringing the terminal rate to 1.75%.
The emerging market story has increasingly shifted from easing toward patience or selective
tightening. In Asia, China's weak domestic demand and persistent property-sector strains continue to
warrant policy support, with AI-related exports providing some offset. We expect a more gradual pace
of Reserve Requirement Ratio cuts alongside targeted monetary and fiscal measures. India faces the

opposite challenge, with elevated food and energy prices and rupee depreciation strengthening the
case for further Reserve Bank of India tightening. In Latin America, peso depreciation and elevated
energy costs pose upside inflation risks for Mexico, although limited exchange-rate pass-through
should allow Banxico to remain on hold while policymakers monitor underlying inflation and inflation
expectations. In Brazil, election-related uncertainty and renewed inflation pressures are likely to delay

further easing, with cuts expected to resume in Q1-2027 once policymakers see more convincing
evidence of disinflation.

### Page 1

#retail #newzealand
First Impressions: NZ retail card spending, October 2026
By Michael Gordon
Senior Economist, Westpac NZ
10:26 November 12 2026 Share
Card spending was surprisingly weak again in October.
Retail spending
Monthly change: -0.7% (prev: -0.5%, Westpac forecast: +1.4%)
Annual change: +0.3% (prev: +0.9%)

### Page 2

Core retail spending (excluding fuel/autos) 
Monthly change: -1.2% (prev: -0.7%) 
Annual change: -0.8% (prev: +0.1%)

Retail spending on credit and debit cards was reportedly down 0.7% in October, a third straight monthly
decline. Spending was down across all categories with the exception of fuel, which saw a 4.8% rise in
spending – and with fuel prices around 10% higher over the month, that still points to a decline in
spending volumes. 
We should note that we have had concerns for some time about the coverage of this data. The monthly
result was again significantly weaker than what we recorded for spending on Westpac-issued credit
and debit cards (ANZ also reported an increase in spending on its own figures). And as a lead indicator
of the quarterly retail survey (which feeds into GDP), it has undershot in every one of the last six
quarters. We don’t suggest that spending is growing strongly by any means – and it is likely the case
that higher fuel prices are crowding out spending elsewhere. But we suspect that the true picture is not
as weak as today’s release suggests. 
A drop in oil prices since the start of November has provided some relief for fuel prices at the pump,
although they remain high. We anticipate that growth in consumer spending volumes will remain
subdued in the near term, with regional variations dependent on exposure to the well-performing export
sector. What happens beyond this year will depend on how the Middle East conflict evolves and how
the RBNZ responds to the rebound in inflation.

Document 358: Global Views_ Downside Growth Risks

Source PDF: all report pdf/Global Views_ Downside Growth Risks.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-10-26
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

Jan Hatzius 
+1(212)902-0394 | jan.hatzius@gs.com 
Goldman Sachs & Co. LLC
Global Views: Downside Growth Risks
26 October 2026 | 6:45AM EDT
1. So far in 2026, global growth has been remarkably unremarkable.  Both the world 
as a whole and the G3 economies are growing in line with our estimates of potential 
GDP .  Our interpretation is that fiscal easing and the positive impulse from the AI 
boom have offset the negative impulse from the war in Iran and the lingering drag 
from higher US tariffs.

2. Our standing global GDP forecasts project more of the same in 2027, as lower 
energy prices offset fading tailwinds from fiscal policy and AI.  But the balance of 
risks is on the downside.  The most immediate concern is that, far from subsiding, 
energy prices have again climbed sharply on fears of renewed Middle East escalation.  
The prices of gasoline, diesel, jet fuel, and European/Asian natural gas are now near 
or even above their March/April highs, with likely negative effects on consumers and 
energy-importing economies more broadly.

3. Although we don’t expect significant fiscal policy changes in 2027, the consequences 
of large-scale deficits—coupled with AI-linked borrowing and central bank hikes—have 
started to show up more clearly in higher longer-term interest rates.  The moves have 
been mostly orderly, but the increase since early 2026 averages a sizable 100bp across 
DM yield curves.  If sustained, we estimate that this increase will subtract a cumulative 
0.6% from GDP via weakness in housing, consumer durables, and business investment.  
(Only part of the drag is incorporated in our standing forecasts because we expect bond 
yields to pull back as central banks deliver fewer hikes than priced in markets.)

Exhibit 2: Oil Price Risks Are Skewed to the Upside

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research

### Page 3

4. The growth impulse from the AI boom was set to peak in late 2026 or early 2027 even 
before concerns around the sustainability of the AI investment boom intensified in 
recent weeks.  Although AI investment is still rising rapidly, we expect the increase as a 
share of GDP to slow over the next year.  Alongside the fading boost from last year’s 
increase in US depreciation allowances, this will weigh on capital spending growth, 
especially in the US.  AI has also boosted US consumer spending growth via a positive 
wealth effect worth an estimated 0.4pp (or 0.25% of GDP) over the past year.  This 
impact is likely to decline over the next year, gradually if stocks perform in line with our 
strategists’ reasonably upbeat expectations but more sharply if AI-related stocks 
continue to decline.

5. Our own base case remains that AI investment will ultimately pay off in terms of higher 
productivity and economywide profits.  However, one key channel for this payoff is labor 
market disruption, which has negative near-term macroeconomic effects.  And while DM 
labor markets still look solid in aggregate, we are starting to see clearer signs that the AI 
impact is spreading beyond the technology sector.  Across nonmanufacturing industries, 
the share of US firms that have adopted AI in regular business functions now shows a 
statistically significant negative correlation with employment growth and a statistically 
significant positive correlation with unemployment.

6. The Fed’s recent signals—explicit from Williams/Jefferson and implicit from Warsh—
effectively rule out a hike on October 28.  But the FOMC still looks very likely to deliver 
another 25bp hike to 4-4.25% on December 9 because spot inflation remains too high.  
And while we continue to expect significant disinflation next year on the back of 
reduced impulses from tariffs, energy prices, and AI-related prices, the risks to our 
forecast are on the upside, most obviously in energy.  Higher inflation could result in

another hike in March, but either way we see renewed cuts in late 2027/early 2028.

7. Like the Fed, the ECB will likely hold rates steady next week and deliver a final hike (to 
2.75%) at the December meeting.  However, the turbulence in the French bond market 
poses somewhat greater downside risks to that call than in the US.  The turbulence has 
tightened financial conditions to a degree that is likely to shave ¼-½pp from annualized 
growth in coming quarters.  And if it intensifies into December, markets might perceive a 
hike as signaling insufficient focus by the ECB on the risk of Euro area fragmentation.  
While the ECB could address this issue by coupling a hike with renewed balance sheet 
expansion, the hurdle for such a move is high, in part because purchases of French 
bonds would inevitably raise concerns about moral hazard with respect to the needed 
fiscal adjustment.

### Page 6

November 5.  Consistent with our view that the UK economy is stronger than widely 
perceived, GDP growth has surprised on the upside this year and the unemployment rate 
declined to 4.7% in August on a 3-month average basis (with the volatile 1-month rate 
plunging to 4.1%).  Rising inflation and the looming household energy price reset in 
January imply a significant risk of further moves, although market pricing of 4+ hikes 
(including November) still looks excessive.  We also expect hikes in Canada and New 
Zealand this week and in Australia next week.

9. Slightly faster than expected growth in Q3 has pushed up our 2026 GDP forecast for 
China to 4.6%.  But most activity indicators are much weaker.  Both property activity and 
fixed asset investment are contracting year-on-year, and while nominal retail sales 
growth picked up to +1.1% in September, large retailers (whose sales are relatively 
better measured) are still posting substantial year-on-year declines.  With domestic 
demand rising slowly at best, sustained GDP growth in the official 4.5-5% target range 
will require ongoing steep increases in the trade surplus.  But the surplus is already at 1% 
of global GDP , and further increases will eat more noticeably into output and 
employment abroad.  Ultimately, China’s trading partners may respond by imposing 
trade barriers that weigh on Chinese exports and GDP , although the measures taken so 
far have been limited.

Exhibit 8: UK Growth Forecasts Have Drifted Higher

Source: Bloomberg, Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research
